VOLUME NOTE · BUILD 2

About This Volume

What this standalone volume holds, and how to read its references.

This is Build 2 of the Tandem Companion: a standalone working volume for For Their Entertainment and Remnia. It carries Part I (how the Companion works) in full, the Part II sections FTE depends on, and all of Part IV, with working pages for IV.10 and IV.25 and a rebuilt working record of the Remnian imperial and royal lineage after IV.11. The copy is the locked Stage 3 Content Master; this volume lays it out and adds working pages. It doesn’t add canon.

Some pages point to sections that live only in the full Tandem Companion. Two conventions keep those pointers honest:

  • Continuity Room and 3SL pointers resolve here. References to VII.1, VII.2, VII.5, VII.7, VII.8, VII.10, VII.12 and Part VII, and to the 3SL sections III.3, III.13–III.16, III.18, III.20 and Part III, lead to the FTE Continuity Room concordance near the end of this volume, which names where the FTE information already sits in these pages.
  • Everything else outside the volume carries a dagger (†). A reference marked † is to a section of the full Tandem Companion that isn’t printed here.
  • Page numbers. “See also” chips give the page where each in-volume section starts.
Corrections applied in this volume

Two author status corrections post-date the Content Master and are applied where the claims appear (IV.2, IV.3). The lineage reconstruction also corrected one status and several source attributions in IV.11, IV.12 and IV.22; each is logged in the QA report with the exact text it replaces. Rulings without a TCD number are listed in Appendix A.

AUTHOR CORRECTION · 2026-10-09 · IV.2 AND IV.3
CANON
Remnia is an empire on its own continental landmass, separated from Somnoria by ocean; the Crown sits at the highest public civil/dynastic level of the Remnian sovereignty structure.
TCD-025 · TCD-041
DRAFTED
Remnia has seventeen provinces.
FTE Beta (ch. 2 and two further passages; IV.2)
DRAFTED
The military structure (the Legion, the Vanguard, the En Guard). TCD-041 is the layered sovereignty framework, not the military’s place in it.
FTE Beta chs. 1, 5, 26
The lineage record

The pages after IV.11 rebuild the Remnian imperial and royal lineage from the sources, claim by claim. Each claim carries its source and the status that source earns. Biology, legal family status, the public record, titles and succession are kept in separate layers. The record is a derived working record, not a new source, and it settles nothing the rulings leave open. The unaudited Imperial Lineage was used only for comparison.

What isn’t in this volume

Part III and Parts V–VII (3SL, ADST, TSWK and the full Continuity Room); the Part II sections FTE doesn’t stand on (II.1, II.3, II.7–II.12); and Appendix D. The appendices printed here are FTE slices: the rulings, conflicts, sources and terms this volume cites.

How This Companion Works

This Part teaches you to trust a page in under ten minutes. Every later Part assumes you know what a status marker permits, which source wins in which domain, and why a character can't know something just because you do.

I.1
ORIENTATION · PART I · HOW THIS COMPANION WORKS

What the Tandem Companion Is

Frame the book as a working writer-room reference, not a wiki or workbook.

The Tandem Companion is the working reference for four novels set in one physical world: 3 Shadowmere Lane, For Their Entertainment, A Dream So Twisted, and The Sea Will Keep. It holds the canon those books have already committed to, the rulings that settled their contradictions, and the open questions they haven't answered yet. It sits beside the manuscript while you draft. It isn't a replacement for it.

The Companion does five jobs. It remembers what's been ruled and drafted so you don't have to reread three generations of files to find out. It distinguishes things a drafter will blur: the Nightmare organization from the ancient entity, Katherine's Frost from Zephyr's, the forensic record of Remy's death from Melena's guilt about it. It connects the books only where a ruling or source connects them. It protects chronology and knowledge, so nobody in 3SL behaves as if they've read ADST. And it catches resurrected ideas, the retired concepts that keep sneaking back because they were once written down with confidence.

It refuses a few jobs on purpose. It won't fill a gap because the gap looks awkward in print. It won't pick the nicer version of a conflict. It won't invent a sigil, a motto, a date, a map coordinate, or a line of scripture to make a page feel finished. It isn't a fan wiki, a marketing synopsis, or a workbook of blank lines waiting for you to do the thinking. When the sources don't know something, the Companion says so plainly, and that sentence is worth more than a confident guess.

I.2
RULE · PART I · HOW THIS COMPANION WORKS

The Manuscript Outranks the Companion

State the single rule that keeps the Companion subordinate to the books.

Drafted prose controls drafted events. If a chapter in the controlling generation shows something happening, it happened, and the Companion records it. If that chapter deliberately changes something the Companion already printed, the Companion changes. A printed page never gets to veto a scene.

That rule has limits that matter as much as the rule. The manuscript controls what it actually shows: POV, scene order, revealed identities, performed magic, relationships, and immediate consequences. It doesn't automatically control national geography, deep history, or magical taxonomy just because a character says something about them. When Silas thinks nobody born in the Underside carries fire, that's a drafted fact about what Silas believes. It becomes world-level canon only if a ruling or the governing worldbuilding agrees. A manuscript can also be wrong by accident. A typo, a stray name from an older generation, or a detail that contradicts a ruling isn't a deliberate change; it's a drafting flag, and the Companion logs it as one instead of rewriting canon around it.

Open and provisional material works the other way. Where the Companion marks something OPEN or SUPPORTED / PROVISIONAL, drafting is allowed to settle it. When a scene commits to an answer on purpose, that commitment becomes the new record, and the Companion's update protocol (I.6) moves the old version to the Legacy Recovery Index rather than deleting it.

GUARDRAIL

Never bend a scene to match a Companion page. If the scene is right and the page is wrong, fix the page. If the scene contradicts an author ruling (a TCD), stop and decide which one you mean; don't let either quietly win.

Why it matters: the Companion is only trustworthy if it can't become a second, rival manuscript. The moment a printed entry starts dictating prose, the books stop being written and start being transcribed from an index.

I.3
SOURCE AUTHORITY · PART I · HOW THIS COMPANION WORKS

How Source Authority Works

Teach the domain-specific ladder so a reader knows why a claim is trusted.

Authority is domain-specific. A source can settle one kind of fact and have no say over another. The 3SL draft is the last word on what happens in Dante Mercer's office in chapter 3; it isn't the last word on how far Crownlight sits above sea level. The Thalara worldbuilding file governs Thalaran geography; it can't overrule a ruling about the Drowning. Every claim in this book is trusted because of the rung it stands on in its own domain.

The authority ladder
RungSource typeWhat it controlsWhat it can't do
1Explicit author rulings (Canon Decision Log, TCD-001 onward)Every domain a ruling addressesNothing outranks it except a later ruling
2Current manuscript pagesWhat happened on-page: POV, scene chronology, revealed identity, performed magic, relationships, immediate consequencesSettle geography, history, or taxonomy by a character's say-so
3Locked constraints (Novel Constraints, as corrected by rulings)Intended premise and undrafted core engineOverride a drafted scene or a later ruling
4Current outlines and chapter plansUndrafted plot, once their generation is confirmedOverwrite anything already drafted
5Current book-specific worldbuilding, character, magic, and institutional filesGeography, institutions, culture, baseline magic, backstory where the manuscript is silentContradict a ruling or an on-page event
6Shared Tandem loreIts claims one at a time, only where they survive reviewServe as a blanket master plan
7Supporting artifacts (timelines, placement tables, genealogy workbooks, poems, quote maps)Their narrow domain, after consistency checksFill gaps outside that domain
8Marketing filesPositioning languageEstablish any canon
SPINE › I.3 HOW SOURCE AUTHORITY WORKS · CONTINUES →I.3 / 1 · 12
SPINE
3SL
FTE
ADST
TSWK
CONTINUITY
I.3 · HOW SOURCE AUTHORITY WORKSCONTINUED
RungSource typeWhat it controlsWhat it can't do
9Legacy drafts and plansCreative history and recovery leadsAnswer a current question
10TKC Companion materialsStructural and philosophical models onlyContribute any Tandem canon, ever

Two rules sit above the ladder. First, the Tandem Source Authority Audit's rulings override conflicting source material wherever they speak. Second, filenames and dates confer nothing. "FINAL," "Official," "New," and a recent year are labels a file picked up along the way. FTE 2025 FINAL is Legacy. New FTE Chapters predates the modern Remnia execution. A later date on the TSWK blueprint doesn't make it a ruling.

Worked example: the Beta versus the 2025 FINAL. FTE 2025 FINAL is a complete 47-chapter manuscript. The FTE Official Beta Reader Copy stops at chapter 29. A reader who trusts completeness picks the FINAL and gets the ending for free. TCD-001 says no: the Beta is the controlling prose generation, and nothing outside it becomes canon until it's rewritten into that generation. The FINAL still earns its shelf space as planning evidence for the unfinished half, but its simple Dyad/Crossblood explanation, its reveal shortcut, its cause for Atlas's deterioration, and its Tiran-only forfeit are all superseded by TCD-049 through TCD-052. The complete file lost to the incomplete one because the incomplete one is the book being written.

Current controlling generations at a glance
BookControls drafted eventsSupports undrafted workStatus of the rest
3SL3 Shadowmere Lane First Draft, 22 chapters32-chapter outline for fixed anchors only; 3SL Character Outlines; 3SL Full WorldbuildingThe outline's chapter map no longer controls (TCD-002)
FTEFTE Official Beta Reader Copy, through ch. 29Chapter 29 fragment and chs. 31–39 (rewrite required); The Challenges FTE Year; 49th Chess Tournament PlacementsEvery pre-Beta generation is Legacy (TCD-001)
ADSTADST, 19 chaptersRevised 17-chapter plan; retained climax components from the older 42-chapter planThe older plan's early execution and ending outcome are superseded (TCD-003, TCD-053)

SOURCE NOTE

The Project copies of several files carry names without the version suffixes the audit used (3 Shadowmere Lane First Draft rather than First Draft(9); ADST rather than ADST(3)). The Companion treats each as the audited generation because its content matches the audit's description. The Imperial Lineage appears in the Project but not in the audit inventory, so it holds no assigned authority until it's classified; nothing in Parts I–III relies on it.

Why it matters: most canon errors in a four-book universe aren't invented facts. They're real facts pulled from the wrong generation or the wrong domain. The ladder is how the Companion answers "which file?" before it answers "what's true?"

SOURCEAudit, Preliminary Domain-Based Source Authority Map; Production Master A2; TCD-001, TCD-002, TCD-003, TCD-010, TCD-012.

I.4
STATUS KEY · PART I · HOW THIS COMPANION WORKS

Reading a Claim: Status Markers

Teach the eight markers and the wording each allows.

A status marker attaches to a claim, not to a page. One dossier page can hold settled canon, a drafted fact, a plan, and an open question side by side, and each keeps its own label. The marker also controls the grammar. A POSSIBLE claim written in confident present tense is a canon error even with the POSSIBLE sitting next to it, because readers believe sentences before they believe badges.

Status key
MarkerMeaningPermitted wordingForbidden wording
CANONEstablished by an author ruling or a controlling source in its domainPlain declarative present tense, with the TCD or source citedHedging that makes settled canon look optional
CURRENT DRAFTED TEXTEstablished on-page in the controlling manuscript generation"On-page, …" · "As drafted, …" · "In ch. 7, …"Treating it as immune to revision
SUPPORTED / PROVISIONALA current plan, bible, blueprint, or artifact that hasn't become drafted canon"The outline plans…" · "The blueprint proposes…" · "The worldbuilding file describes…"Present-tense narration of events as though they happened
POSSIBLEA live candidate or recovery idea, not approved"A candidate is…" · "One viable reading…" · "If recovered, …""Is," "will," or "does" about the story world
OPENUnresolved; an author decision is needed if and when drafting forces itState what's fixed, then name exactly what isn'tFilling the gap with a plausible answer
CONFLICTCurrent sources disagree and no ruling settles itName both sources and both claimsPicking the nicer version silently

I.4 · READING A CLAIM: STATUS MARKERSCONTINUED
MarkerMeaningPermitted wordingForbidden wording
RESEARCHNeeds outside factual researchState the research questionPresenting a guess as fact
LEGACY / SUPERSEDEDCreative history that no longer controlsPast tense, source named: "The 2025 FINAL had…"Using it to answer a current question
How to read a claim: six worked examples
Claim as printedStatusWhy it's labeled this way
Katherine Nicholas is the current Tournament's Conqueror.CANON · TCD-034An author ruling fixes it, so it takes plain present tense even though the scene that earns it isn't drafted yet. The ruling controls the outcome; the manuscript still has to build the causal path.
On-page, Katherine's Examination puts her through severe Fire trauma and still produces only Frost.CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT · FTE Beta; TCD-058The Beta shows it. TCD-058 later explains why it happens (dominant Frost configuration, no ordinary stress trigger), but the event itself is anchored in drafted prose.
The July 2026 blueprint plans Azrine's ordination near the end of Book One.SUPPORTED / PROVISIONAL · blueprint, per audit HC-061The most detailed TSWK plan proposes it. No ruling has adopted it, so it's written as a plan, never as "Azrine is ordained."
The Conduit Trials' final canon name hasn't been chosen.OPEN · TCD-008The concept and mechanics are current. The name is a working title, and every page that uses it flags it.
The blueprint's chapter 29 credits "Cael Dren" with designing the sacrificial route and First Anchor, while its chapters 38–39 identify twelve-year-old Caelen as the first sacrificed Anchor.CONFLICT · HC-059Two passages in one plan disagree. The Companion names both and protects TCD-064's twelve-year-old Caelen without deciding who designed the route.

I.4 · READING A CLAIM: STATUS MARKERSCONTINUED
Claim as printedStatusWhy it's labeled this way
The 2025-era lore treated the Echo Gate as the singular sealed crossing between Remnia and Somnoria.LEGACY / SUPERSEDED · TCD-027Past tense, source named. Its seal, spiral or mirrored glyph, and ancient-crossing imagery may come back only as POSSIBLE.

RESEARCH appears when a scene needs real-world fact the sources can't supply. The clearest current case is the Remy death scene: once the weapon and wound are chosen, the blood-loss timing Melena works against is a research question, not a canon question (TCD-063 leaves weapon and anatomy open).

Page furniture you'll see throughout
  • Source — lines close each section. They're margin provenance in the manuscript and become small-type margin notes in layout.
  • GUARDRAIL boxes hold locked constraints a drafter could break without noticing.
  • NOT THE SAME AS callouts separate paired terms readers merge.
  • OPEN DEVELOPMENT boxes carry four lines: Fixed / Undecided / Why it matters / Decide by.
  • TANDEM CANON DECISION blocks appear only where a page genuinely can't proceed.
  • See also lines point to related sections by number.

Why it matters: the status system is the reason this book can be trusted at 2 a.m. in the middle of a chapter. If a claim has no marker and no qualifying grammar, you're meant to be able to rely on it without checking.

SOURCEProduction Master A3, V6; TCD-008, TCD-027, TCD-034, TCD-058, TCD-063, TCD-064; Audit HC-059, HC-061.

I.5
EXPLAINER · PART I · HOW THIS COMPANION WORKS

Knowledge States

Prevent backward knowledge promotion.

No later revelation travels backward into an earlier head. That sounds obvious until the fourth book is drafted and a character in the first one starts making choices that only make sense if she's read the Canon Decision Log. The Companion prevents that by keeping seven lanes of knowledge apart wherever a secret, identity, reveal, or piece of history is involved.

The seven lanes
LaneWhat it recordsExample of the lane in use
Objective truthWhat is true in the universeThe gang leader's wound kills Remy (TCD-063).
Author knowledgeWhat Danyelle has ruled or knows, whether or not anyone on-page doesKatherine and Melena are paternal half-sisters through Tobias (TCD-040), long before either sister proves it.
Reader knowledgeWhat a reader knows at a specific point in a specific bookTracked per chapter in each book's knowledge ledger; never assumed from author knowledge.
Character knowledgeWhat a named character knows, and from whenMelena enters the Challenge Six Duel with substantial suspicion about Katherine and no proof (TCD-050).
Character beliefWhat a character thinks is true when it's wrong or incompleteEarly Reverie believes she's an illegal, unlicensed Dreamwalker with a rare gift; she's also a natural conduit (TCD-029).
Institutional / public versionWhat Houses, the Sanctum, the Synod, Skyhold, schools, or governments teach or recordThe Sanctum's likely "Dyad" label for Katherine, which is incomplete (TCD-049).
Suppressed / rewrittenWhat's sealed, censored, misfiled, mythologized, redacted, or lostHouse Dren's willing-adult First Anchor mythology versus twelve-year-old Caelen (TCD-064).

The six levels of cross-land knowledge. Knowing another land exists doesn't mean knowing where it is, how its magic works, how to reach it, what its history is, or how the four lands actually relate. TCD-016 separates these into six levels, and none implies another:

SPINE › I.5 KNOWLEDGE STATES · CONTINUES →I.5 / 1 · 18
SPINE
3SL
FTE
ADST
TSWK
CONTINUITY
I.5 · KNOWLEDGE STATESCONTINUED
  1. Existence — the land is real.
  2. Location — where it is.
  3. Functional — how its people, magic, or institutions work.
  4. Route — how to get there.
  5. Historical — what happened between the lands.
  6. True Tandem knowledge — the objective relationship among all four.

A drafted 3SL example shows why the ladder matters. In chapter 11, Remy jokes that the only place Melena can't reach the blackroot is Somnoria, and Silas hopes she won't ask what Somnoria is. On-page, that establishes that both men know Somnoria by name. It doesn't establish that either knows where it is, how to get there, or anything about dream magic. In chapter 3, Silas reads Melena's fire as proof she's Remnian because "fire belonged to Remnia." That's functional knowledge about one feature of Remnian magic, held as Silas's belief, and it says nothing about what the Synod teaches or what Silas knows of Remnian history. CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT (3SL chs. 3, 11).

Worked example: two clocks for one missing man. Elias Lockwood and Professor Alexander Thorne are one person with two public lives, and the two lives go missing on different schedules.

LaneElias Lockwood / Professor Alexander ThorneStatus
Objective truthElias voluntarily created and maintained Thorne as an established Somnorian researcher and instructor for years. The Nightmare organization targets Thorne's boundary/Network research and exploits the vulnerability it creates, compromising his dream and magical functioning so that ordinary return to Remnia becomes unsafe or impossible.CANON TCD-032, TCD-062
Author knowledgeThe failure-to-return cause is fixed. The exact compromise technique, first crossing, alias credentials, helpers, later captivity, and physical status are not.OPEN
Institutional / public version, RemniaRemnia notices Elias missing first, because he fails to return on pattern.CANON TCD-062

I.5 · KNOWLEDGE STATESCONTINUED
LaneElias Lockwood / Professor Alexander ThorneStatus
Institutional / public version, SomnoriaThorne initially remains publicly present but deteriorating; his Somnorian disappearance comes later.CANON TCD-062

Why it matters: the two clocks are the whole trick of this plotline. Remnia is already searching while Somnoria still sees Thorne teaching. Collapse the lanes and one country reacts to news it hasn't received.

GUARDRAIL

In-world language survives as perspective, never as cosmology. A Somnorian calling Remnia "another world" is Somnorian framing (TCD-015). House Aether calling the Prism a "cage" is House Aether's interpretation (TCD-038). Caelum calling Melena his "asset" is Caelum's framing (TCD-043).

SOURCETCD-015, TCD-016, TCD-029, TCD-032, TCD-038, TCD-040, TCD-043, TCD-049, TCD-050, TCD-062, TCD-063, TCD-064; Production Master A5; 3SL draft chs. 3, 11.

I.6
PROTOCOL · PART I · HOW THIS COMPANION WORKS

Open Canon, Decisions, and Updates

Show how open material is displayed and how rulings enter the book.

Open stays open. The Companion doesn't close a question because a page looks unfinished with it, and it doesn't ask for a ruling just because the book reached that topic. It asks only when a page, a scene, or a publication-level commitment can't be written accurately without an answer.

The OPEN DEVELOPMENT box. Wherever an open point sits beside settled canon, it gets a four-line box. Here's a live one, reproduced from Part II:

OPEN DEVELOPMENT · THE REMNIA–UNDERSIDE CROSSING · OPEN
FIXEDA current crossing exists near the London Town edge, in the no-man's-land associated with London Town, Old Prosperity, and Alea. It's physical-magical transit, not a portal. The singular Echo Gate is Legacy. (TCD-017, TCD-027)
UNDECIDEDIts precise map coordinate, and whether it arrives directly in Shadowmere, Tier 7.
WHY IT MATTERSArrival tier decides who controls the Underside end, who can trace a crossing, and how Melena's route from Remnia into Shadowmere reads on-page.
DECIDE BYWhen Danyelle's Remnia map is reviewed, or earlier if a 3SL scene has to show the arrival point.

The "Decide by" line is the useful one. It names the actual drafting moment that forces the answer. If nothing forces it, the line says "safe to defer," and that's a complete answer.

The TANDEM CANON DECISION block. When a page truly can't proceed, the Companion uses this format and nothing looser: the exact issue, the sources creating it, options A through D where the evidence offers them, E for "Something else / I want to explain," one line of implication under each option, and a labeled recommendation only when the evidence strongly favors one path. No more than five at once; one to three is better. A recommendation is never canon until ruled.

SPINE › I.6 OPEN CANON, DECISIONS, AND UPDATES · CONTINUES →I.6 / 1 · 21
SPINE

FTE
ADST

CONTINUITY
I.6 · OPEN CANON, DECISIONS, AND UPDATESCONTINUED
TANDEM CANON DECISION — SPECIMEN (FORMAT ONLY; NOT ASKED NOW)

Issue: Who designed First Shellward's sacrificial route and First Anchor?

Sources: July 2026 TSWK blueprint ch. 29 ("Cael Dren" as designer) versus chs. 38–39 (twelve-year-old Caelen as first sacrificed Anchor); TCD-064.

A. Forged credit. House record credits a figure who didn't design it. Implication: the tablet becomes evidence of rewritten history.

B. Distinct adult designer. Cael Dren and Caelen are different people. Implication: two names need separate provenance.

C. Drafting error. The blueprint conflated names. Implication: one passage is corrected; no in-world lie.

E. Something else / I want to explain.

Recommendation: None. The evidence doesn't strongly favor one option, and HC-059 is deferred to the TSWK draft.

How a ruling enters the book. When a new ruling lands or drafted prose deliberately changes something:

  1. Log it in the Canon Decision Log with its date, what it supersedes, and its downstream implications.
  2. Update the High-Impact Conflict Register if it resolves or opens a conflict.
  3. Update every Companion page listed in the ruling's affected-sections field.
  4. Move superseded claims to the Legacy Recovery Index rather than deleting them.
  5. Re-run the integrity checks on every touched page: no Legacy claim presented as canon, no POSSIBLE or SUPPORTED claim in declarative story-world tense, no conflict silently harmonized, no knowledge promoted backward, no symmetry assumed across the four lands, no duplicate or contradictory table, no invented canon, no open question phrased as settled, no TKC content imported.
GUARDRAIL

Asking a question assigns no TCD number. The audit's proposed TCD-065 through TCD-074 were never answered or adopted, so none of them is canon and none of those numbers is reserved.

I.6 · OPEN CANON, DECISIONS, AND UPDATESCONTINUED

Why it matters: an open question that's clearly marked is safe to draft around. An open question that's been quietly answered by whoever wrote the page last is how three versions of the same backstory end up in print.

I.7
CHECKLIST · PART I · HOW THIS COMPANION WORKS

Using the Companion While Drafting

Make the book fast to use mid-scene.

Start with the book's draft-forward packet, not the front of the Companion. Each dossier ends with pages built to sit open beside the manuscript: III.19 for 3SL, IV.24 for FTE, V.17 for ADST, VI.13 for TSWK. They condense the locked beats, the current knowledge states, and the scene-level open choices, and they cross-reference the full sections behind them.

Before a scene
  • Check the packet for any GUARDRAIL that touches the characters or magic in the scene.
  • Check the knowledge ledger for what each POV character knows at this chapter, not at the end of the book.
  • Check whether the scene touches a CANON ruling. If it does, the outcome is fixed and the scene's job is to earn it.
  • Check whether the scene touches an OPEN item. If the open item doesn't affect this scene, keep drafting.
While drafting
  • An open question that doesn't touch the next scene doesn't stop the next scene.
  • If the scene needs an answer to an open item, make the choice in prose and flag it. You don't need a ruling first; you need a record afterward.
  • If the scene seems to need something that contradicts a ruling, stop. That's the one case where the draft waits.
  • Don't pull a fact from a Legacy file to fill a gap, however well it fits. Check the Legacy Recovery Index; if the idea is listed there as recoverable, it comes back as POSSIBLE and gets ruled on like anything new.
SPINE › I.7 USING THE COMPANION WHILE DRAFTING · CONTINUES →I.7 / 1 · 24
SPINE
3SL
FTE
ADST
TSWK
CONTINUITY
I.7 · USING THE COMPANION WHILE DRAFTINGCONTINUED
After the scene
  • If the prose settled an open item on purpose, log it through the update protocol (I.6).
  • If the prose changed a knowledge state, update the book's knowledge ledger.
  • If the prose introduced a new name, place, route, or rule, add it to the relevant dossier with its chapter as provenance.

Navigation conventions. Sections are numbered Part.section (III.8, VII.5), so insertions don't renumber the book. "See also" lines at each section's foot point outward. Rulings are cited by TCD number, conflicts by HC number, and manuscript facts by book and chapter.

Why it matters: the Companion should cost you seconds mid-scene, not an evening. If using it slows drafting down more than it protects the draft, the page is built wrong, and that's a Companion problem to fix.

SOURCEProduction Master I.7, A8; Audit, Architecture Handoff.

II.2
SCHEMATIC · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

Macro Geography

Place the lands relative to each other without inventing cartography.

The four lands occupy multiple major landmasses and regions in one world. CANON TCD-025. Four placements are fixed:

  • Remnia and Somnoria are separate continental landmasses with ocean between them.
  • The Underside attaches to the Zenithia side, or corner, of Remnia's broader geography and descends from there. CANON TCD-025, TCD-026
  • Thalara, primarily underwater, lies farther outward in the island and sea region.
  • Relative position is described by a Spain / France / Italy / Greek-islands comparison. That comparison controls relative placement only.
GUARDRAIL
What the analogy does not mean.

It doesn't import real borders, coastlines, scale, distances, climate, culture, languages, or history. It doesn't make any land a disguised version of a real country. It doesn't settle which way anything faces on a map. It tells you roughly where the lands sit in relation to one another, and nothing more.

The Underside's placement deserves its own sentence because it's the easiest to get wrong. It isn't a buried continent lying flat beneath Remnia. It's a vertically tiered nation that begins at or near the surface off Zenithia's side and descends. Its top tiers, Crownlight and Dawnveil, are above ground; by Ironcross the descent is fully subterranean. CANON TCD-026. Part III builds the full cross-section.

SPINE › II.2 MACRO GEOGRAPHY · CONTINUES →II.2 / 1 · 27

II.2 · MACRO GEOGRAPHYCONTINUED

Schematic, not a map. Until Danyelle's canonical Remnia map is reviewed, the Companion uses a relative-position schematic: two continental masses separated by ocean, the Underside marked as a descending column off one side of Remnia near Zenithia, and Thalara marked as a sea region farther out with its territory below the surface. The schematic carries no coastlines, scale bar, compass rose, or place names beyond these.

SCHEMATIC · PLACEHOLDER
Relative-position schematic pending review of Danyelle’s canonical Remnia map.
No coastlines, scale, compass, or place names are drawn in this volume.
OPEN DEVELOPMENT · MACRO CARTOGRAPHY · OPEN
FIXEDOne world; the four placements above; the Underside's vertical attachment off Zenithia's side (TCD-015, TCD-025, TCD-026).
UNDECIDEDCoastlines, names of landmasses and oceans, scale, distances, islands, and borders. Zenithia's exact political classification is confirmed against the Remnian province index in Part IV rather than assumed here.
WHY IT MATTERSDistance sets travel time, and travel time sets how long news takes to cross (VII.2).
DECIDE BYRemnia layer when the Remnia map is reviewed; other layers safe to defer until a scene needs a distance.

Why it matters: geography is where cross-book continuity breaks quietly. A character who reaches Somnoria "by nightfall" has just committed the Companion to a distance, whether anyone meant to.

II.4
EXPLAINER · LADDER · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

Knowledge of the Other Lands

Kill universal-awareness shortcuts.

There's no universal answer to "do people know about the other lands?" Awareness varies by nation, class, institution, profession, network, person, and time. CANON TCD-016. A smuggler, a Sanctum archivist, and a miner's kid in the same land can hold three different pictures, and all three can change between chapter 5 and chapter 25.

The six levels from I.5 do the sorting: existence, location, functional, route, historical, and true Tandem knowledge. None implies another. Someone can know Somnoria's name and nothing about where it is. Someone can use a route without knowing what's on the far side of the boundary it crosses. Someone can know a land's history as their own institution teaches it, which may be the least reliable version available.

Four kinds of source feed a character's picture, and the Companion tracks them separately: what their institution teaches or officially claims, what their network passes around as rumor or trade knowledge, what's been suppressed or rewritten, and what they've learned firsthand. An error counts as knowledge for drafting purposes. A character acts on what she believes, and the Companion records the belief with its lane label.

What the drafted pages already establish. These are seed entries for the full matrix in VII.5. Every row is dated to its chapter; none is a claim about what the character's whole society knows.

CharacterChapterKnowledge on-pageLevelStatus
Silas Graves3SL ch. 3Reads Melena's fire as proof she's Remnian; believes nobody born in the Underside carries fireFunctional (as belief)CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT
Silas Graves3SL ch. 11Knows Somnoria by name; hopes Melena won't ask what it isExistenceCURRENT DRAFTED TEXT

GUARDRAIL

One character's knowledge never becomes their land's knowledge. Dante knowing Remnian politics says nothing about what the Synod teaches, and the Synod's teaching says nothing about what Gutterlight knows.

Why it matters: cross-land knowledge is the series' pressure valve. When isolation holds, each book can stay intimate and local. When knowledge starts leaking, the leak itself is plot, and it only reads as plot if the Companion knows exactly who knew what before.

II.5
EXPLAINER · TABLE · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

Shared Magical Ancestry

Explain shared deep origin without making magics interchangeable.

All four modern magical traditions ultimately draw from one primordial magical source or substrate that predates the modern division of the Tandem world. CANON TCD-035. The Divide didn't create four unrelated magics. Geography, environment, bodies, bloodlines, culture, survival, history, and institutions reshaped one shared power into systems that are now genuinely distinct, with their own access, costs, classifications, and cultural explanations.

Both halves of that ruling carry weight. The shared root means cross-land resonance is possible without anyone inventing a coincidence. The genuine distinctness means a Somnorian dream technique doesn't work in Remnia just because both descend from the same source, and a Thalaran seal cadence isn't a Silencecraft seal with a different accent. Long isolation after the Divide accelerated the specialization. CANON TCD-045

The four modern branches
LandModern branchKnown practices and termsWho classifies itFalse equivalence to avoid
The UndersideLife, death, grief, and memory magic, formally organized as the Six DisciplinesThanomancy, Lamentcraft, Silencecraft, Underchant Cant, Funereal Engineering, Mycelial Memory (SUPPORTED / PROVISIONAL, 3SL Full Worldbuilding). On-page: Silencecraft and Silence seals, Quietcasters, Shrouds, funeral and grief thread, Hush, Underchant markings (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT, 3SL chs. 1–12). Graveshand is inherited Graves-family magic outside the Six Disciplines. CANON TCD-004The Synod regulates and enforces (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT)Graveshand is not a seventh Discipline.

NOT THE SAME AS
repeated nouns across branches.

Several words recur across the four magics, and none of them proves a shared mechanism. "Tether" names Silas and Isola's involuntary bond, the death tethers the Pit Wardens search for, and the tether-lines Silencecraft's Severing cuts. "Anchor" names Thalara's Anchor Verse, House Dren's Anchor program, and the ADST organization's living conduit/anchor configuration. "Severing" in the Silencecraft section of the worldbuilding and Graveshand Severance in 3SL are separate things until a source joins them. Each term is defined inside its own branch.

II.5 · SHARED MAGICAL ANCESTRYCONTINUED

Resemblance isn't inheritance. The Underside treats memory as currency and names as anchors; Thalara's sea began holding memory after the Drowning, and shells hold impressions. The rhyme is real on the page. It isn't evidence of a shared mechanism, a shared origin event, or a transfer between lands. CANON Production Master A6

OPEN DEVELOPMENT · THE PRIMORDIAL LAYER · OPEN
FIXEDOne shared substrate; four genuinely distinct modern branches; isolation after the Divide accelerated specialization (TCD-035, TCD-045).
UNDECIDEDWhat the substrate was, how it was used before the Divide, the mechanism of divergence, which old principles survive in which branch, how branches interact when they meet, and which culture's theory of magic is most historically accurate.
WHY IT MATTERSKatherine's ancestral reversion (TCD-049) and every future cross-branch encounter lean on this layer.
DECIDE BYSafe to defer until a scene has to explain a cross-branch interaction rather than just show one.

Why it matters: shared ancestry gives the series its deep logic, and false equivalence is the fastest way to ruin it. When two lands' magics meet on-page, the scene should feel like two dialects of a lost language, mutually strange, not two brands of the same tool.

II.6
EXPLAINER · NOT THE SAME AS · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

Crossblood: A Label, Not a Biology

Stop one institutional theory from becoming taxonomy.

"Crossblood" is an institutional, scholarly, political, or historical label for people believed to carry inheritance associated with more than one separated Tandem tradition. It isn't an objectively correct universal biological category. CANON TCD-036. Institutions may define it differently through incomplete theory, bloodline politics, fear, or propaganda, and the label never establishes what a person's magic objectively is.

The older two-world lore treated Crossbloods as a settled pre-fracture biology and offered a candidate list that grouped Katherine, Cassius, and Zephyr. That list is LEGACY / SUPERSEDED. Its three names don't share an anomaly: the rulings now explain Katherine and Zephyr in incompatible ways, and nothing established about Cassius puts him in either of their categories.

NOT THE SAME AS

Rare, ancient, hybrid, inherited, or anomalous magic is not Crossblood magic. "Crossblood" is a claim someone makes about a person. Whether it's true is a separate question, answered case by case.

Case table. Every row stays unclassified until a case earns evidence. The "what's actually established" column shows why each person needs individual review instead of a shared label.

PersonWhat's actually established about their magicCrossblood status
Katherine NicholasAncestral reversion to a less-specialized earlier Remnian elemental inheritance; Frost-dominant, with Fire as a rerouted alternative expression. CANON TCD-049, TCD-058OPEN · no case evidence; her ruled anatomy is a Remnian ancestral inheritance
ZephyrA genuine modern Fire/Frost Dyad. CANON TCD-049OPEN · no case evidence
Cassius AetherHouse Aether heir; his Labyrinth scar regulates bond synchronization. CANON TCD-030OPEN · no case evidence

WHY IT MATTERSA label with consequences is a plot engine. Someone called Crossblood by the Sanctum faces different stakes than someone called Crossblood by a Somnorian scholar.
DECIDE BYThe first scene in which an institution applies the label to a named character.

Why it matters: a catch-all label is seductive because it explains everything unusual at once. That's exactly what makes it dangerous in a series built on distinct magics. The Companion lets characters and institutions use the word. It doesn't let the narration believe them.

II.13
COMPARISON · VARIANT TEXTS · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

The Shattered Veil and Its Descendants

Separate author-level text from in-world textual history.

A complete Prophecy of the Shattered Veil exists at the author and universe level. CANON TCD-006. No intact, authoritative master copy of it is presently known in-world. CANON TCD-047. What the four lands have instead are four divergent historical descendants: Remnia's Breathbound Law, Somnoria's Dreamwalker's Writ, the Underside's Mourning Verse, and Thalara's Drowned Canticle. They aren't four physical pieces of one document. Nobody can assemble the prophecy by collecting them.

What characters can do is reconstruct. Comparing the variants against historical, magical, material, and testimonial evidence can recover wording, underlying truth, or both. No national version is presumed untouched. CANON TCD-047

The author-level text. Reproduced exactly from The Poems. This is universe canon. It isn't in any character's hands.

```text

The Prophecy of the Shattered Veil

Before the stars were taught to burn,

Before the tide was told to turn,

A voice was born in blood and breath—

A crown was forged, a vow to Death.

The Order rose with chains of grace,

To bind the world in rightful place.

But power kept is power sown,

And rot will bloom in gardens grown.

They danced beneath a gilded stage,

Their laughter hiding quiet rage.

SPINE › II.13 THE SHATTERED VEIL AND ITS DESCENDANTS · CONTINUES →II.13 / 1 · 37
SPINE
3SL
FTE
ADST
TSWK
CONTINUITY
II.13 · THE SHATTERED VEIL AND ITS DESCENDANTSCONTINUED

They begged the flame to make things right,

Then fed it truth and turned from light.

They whispered wrongs they dared not speak,

And carved their grief into the weak.

They rose with crowns not earned but given—

A curse beneath their blood was written.

A dream unspooled across the sky,

A mirror cracked, a perfect lie.

A fate once fractured found its thread,

But stitched its hope through knots of dread.

These hollowed walls, now wrapped in mold,

Remember what they can't behold.

And from the ashes, sharp and bare—

The truth still waits, but never fair.

Four truths were cast to four-born lands,

Each gripped by time's decaying hands.

One held the storm with breath and bone—

Their names were laws, their heirs were known.

One broke the night to mine their dreams,

And built their gods from shattered schemes.

One walked where waves devoured the past,

With songs too cursed to ever last.

One slept beneath the smoking stone,

And taught the dead to die alone.

CONTINUITY
II.13 · THE SHATTERED VEIL AND ITS DESCENDANTSCONTINUED

Four branches bent by shadow's thread,

Each blind to how the roots had bled.

When time unravels spine from soul,

When suns go dark and rivers toll,

When every oath is stripped of name,

And kin bear crowns they cannot claim—

The veil will split. The mask will fall.

No land will thrive. No voice will call.

But in the ruin, cracked and cold,

A single page remains to hold.

Not war, nor flame, nor faith will win—

But what was lost remembered in.

And those who rise with open eyes

Will curse the stars—and still survive.

```

(In the source file, "remembered" in the final stanza is italicized.)

Four witnesses. The comparison below describes each text as a document. It doesn't claim what any line means in the story world.

The Drowned Canticle is Thalara's Shattered Veil descendant, and it survives within or alongside the broader Seal Song tradition as a historical and prophetic strand. It isn't the whole Seal Song. CANON TCD-028

GUARDRAIL
Readings are not rulings.

The master's four "one…" couplets invite an obvious land-by-land mapping. No ruling confirms it; treat any mapping as POSSIBLE. The Canticle's north/east/south/west lines are not cartography and don't set any land's compass position (II.2). "The Order" in the master text isn't identified with any institution in any book. Nothing in these texts may be paraphrased into new verse and presented as canon.

II.14
EXPLAINER · CAN/CANNOT · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

The Elders' Prayer

Define one inherited working across cultures.

The Elders' Prayer is one inherited magical working that survives in more than one culture under different language and ritual expression. CANON TCD-023. Its objective function is specific: it restores a living person's viable magical and life pattern when that pattern has been severed, corrupted, or catastrophically dysregulated. CANON TCD-057. Restored magical continuity can stabilize secondary bodily functions and call someone back from active dying, as long as a viable living pattern remains.

It has hard edges. It can't resurrect completed death. It can't regenerate destroyed anatomy. CANON TCD-057

What the Prayer can and can't do
CanCan't
Restore a viable living magical/life pattern that's been severed, corrupted, or catastrophically dysregulatedResurrect someone whose death is complete
Stabilize secondary bodily functions when magical disruption is causalRegenerate destroyed anatomy. Isola's leg remains ruined despite life-saving stabilization.
Call someone back from active dying while a viable pattern remainsRedistribute a burden automatically and universally from one person to another
Work through different cultural presentations with the same objective functionServe as a Frost-only ritual

Two performances, about two years apart. Selah and Katherine perform the same inherited working independently, roughly two years apart. CANON TCD-048

Selah WraithmoorKatherine Nicholas
Book3SLFTE
RecipientsSilas and IsolaAtlas
Cultural formUnderside presentationRemnian presentation

GUARDRAIL
No echo.

Selah's act leaves no specific echo, reservoir, transmitted route, or power that Katherine later accesses. The two performances are connected by shared magical ancestry and ritual tradition, not by causation. Katherine doesn't need to know Selah exists, or that the Prayer has an Underside form, or its history. (TCD-023, TCD-048)

NOT THE SAME AS

Selah's cold, which the 3SL draft shows fogging metal and spreading frost across a table (chs. 2, 6, 12), isn't evidence that the Prayer is a Frost working or that Selah and Katherine share an elemental affinity. The Frost-only reading of the ritual is superseded (TCD-057). Similar imagery isn't evidence.

OPEN DEVELOPMENT · THE PRAYER'S MECHANICS · OPEN
FIXEDObjective function; the death boundary; the anatomy boundary; two independent performances; no direct causation (TCD-023, TCD-048, TCD-057).
UNDECIDEDEligibility; what serves as the original-pattern reference; whether a bond or physical contact is required; the exact death threshold; the caster's cost; the transmission history; whether and how the working survives in Somnoria and Thalara; who recognizes it across cultures.
WHY IT MATTERSKatherine's performance for Atlas has to obey the same limits Selah's did. If Atlas's injury has already destroyed anatomy, the Prayer can stabilize him but can't make him whole.
DECIDE BYBefore the FTE endgame drafts Katherine's performance; caster cost before either performance is revised.

II.15
MISREADING TABLE · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

Recurring Institutional Misreadings

Collect the ruled cases where an institution's label diverges from objective truth, so the pattern is visible.

Across all four books, institutions and powerful people keep naming things they don't fully understand, and the names outlive the misunderstanding. The rulings have already caught enough of these that the pattern deserves its own page. Each row below is a case where a label circulating in-world diverges from objective truth.

Why the pattern matters for drafting: an institutional misreading is a knowledge state with a budget and an enforcement arm. The Sanctum doesn't just believe Katherine is a Dyad; it tests, files, and treats her as one. When the truth surfaces, the scene isn't only a revelation. It's an institution discovering that its own paperwork was wrong, and deciding what to do about the people who saw.

GUARDRAIL

Characters and institutions may use these labels freely in dialogue, documents, and POV narration filtered through them. The Companion's own narration never adopts them.

UNDECIDEDMotive, where unruled: whether each misreading is honest error, convenient simplification, or deliberate suppression.
WHY IT MATTERSAn honest error gets corrected when evidence arrives. A deliberate one gets defended.
DECIDE BYBefore each institution's misreading is challenged on-page.

II.16
TIMELINE · PART II · THE SHARED TANDEM SPINE

Shared Chronology

Place the books in time without chapter lockstep.

The books are placed in time by macro windows, not by chapter lockstep. CANON TCD-013. 3SL happens about two years before the main concurrent period. ADST begins when Elias goes missing, and FTE begins a couple of weeks later. The two then run largely concurrently and end around the same time, without requiring their chapters to line up one for one.

TSWK and the next Shadowmere installment form the next concurrent window. They begin directly after FTE and ADST, with no meaningful gap of months or years; a few days or small weeks for logistics are fine. They overlap each other substantially without needing identical opening dates. CANON TCD-014, TCD-024, TCD-054. The handoff is rolling: conditions for the later plots may already be active during late FTE and ADST, and still-later stories may begin activating before the prior pair fully resolves.

Four bands per book. Every book is tracked by four separate dates, because "when does it happen?" has four answers.

BookBackground activationNarrative openingClimaxResolution
3SLOPENAbout two years before the FTE/ADST window; exact date OPENRemy's death and Melena's catastrophic Fire; Tiran's cleanup follows (TCD-002, TCD-063)Silas and Isola on separate immediate paths (TCD-044)
ADSTElias's years-long double life as Thorne; the organization's compromise of his research and dream functioning (TCD-032, TCD-062)When Elias goes missing (TCD-013)The Nightmare organization's attempted Network rupture; the first established Convergence threshold, T0 (TCD-056, TCD-060)Around the same time as FTE's (TCD-013)

Temporal proximity isn't causation. Books that overlap in time don't cause each other's events unless a source says so. Local conflicts in TSWK and the next Shadowmere book stay independently rooted (TCD-054).

WHY IT MATTERSInformation travels at the speed of routes. Without dates, the Companion can't say whether a character could have heard news by a given chapter (VII.2).

IV
PART IV · BUILD 2

For Their Entertainment / Remnia

FTE is the book with the most canon fixed ahead of its own pages. The Beta has drafted twenty-eight and a half chapters; the rulings have already decided who wins, how, why London Town forfeits, what Katherine's magic is, how the sisters find each other, and where Atlas's injury comes from. What the rulings haven't done is write the scenes. This dossier holds both halves: the drafted book as it stands, and the ruled endgame it still has to reach.

SOURCE NOTE FOR ALL OF PART IV

The FTE Official Beta Reader Copy was read by chapter map and targeted passage search, not in one linear pass; chapter placements below come from that map and should be verified in a full read. Chapter 29.docx, FTE 31–39, The Challenges FTE Year, 49th Chess Tournament Placements, FTE Elemental Factions, Welcome to Remnia, and Character Bible were read in full. Files the audit inventories but that aren't in this Project: FTE 2025 FINAL, the late chs. 44–48 plan, Chess Tournament Guidelines and Schedules, Education System in Old Prosperity, Events Leading Up To Novel Start, both FTE Remnian Timelines, Notable Figures, The Royal Lineage, The Remnian Provinces, and Novel Constraints. Where Part IV mentions them, it relies on the audit's record, not the file. (Build 2 note, 2026-10-09: FTE 2025 FINAL, both FTE Remnian Timelines, and Full 4 Series Flesh Out have since been supplied. The IV.11 lineage record uses them claim by claim, and IV.11, IV.12, and IV.22 now cite the Before Story Start timeline where they had relied on the audit's record or the unaudited Imperial Lineage.) The same goes for New Opening, which the audit and Production Master carry as a live alternative to the Beta's opening CONFLICT; it isn't in this Project, so the Companion can't compare the two.

Book Identity and Source Status

Separate canonized prose from usable development.

The Beta is the book. CANON TCD-001. Everything outside it, including the two newest development files, has to be rewritten into the Beta's generation before it becomes canon. That rule does real work in FTE, because the development chapters are good, specific, and in places flatly contradict rulings made after they were written.

IV.2
PLACE DOSSIER · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Remnia at a Glance

Orient the reader in the Empire.
STATUS · AUTHOR CORRECTION 2026-10-09
CANON
An empire on its own continental landmass, separated from Somnoria by ocean; the Crown at the highest public civil/dynastic level of the sovereignty structure.
TCD-025 · TCD-041
DRAFTED
Seventeen provinces.
FTE Beta

Remnia is an empire on its own continental landmass, separated from Somnoria by ocean; the Crown sits at the highest public civil/dynastic level of the Remnian sovereignty structure. CANON TCD-025, TCD-041. The Beta gives it seventeen provinces and confirms the count in three places: Melena's cover story is a tour of "all seventeen" provinces (ch. 2), the Delegation hall has seventeen archways "one for each province of the Empire", and seventeen Sanctum officials attend the Maze. (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT) Its years are counted ER.

The Empire's borders are closed. Caelum says so in ch. 2 ("The Remnian Empire may have closed its borders, but there are still ways through"), and Melena, until he tells her otherwise, believes "There's nothing outside of Remnia anymore." That line is a character belief, dated to ch. 2, not a statement of what Remnia teaches (II.4).

Places on the page. Only what the sources actually put somewhere.

IV.2 · REMNIA AT A GLANCECONTINUED
KNOWLEDGE FLAG
How does Melena classify Shadowmere?

She has lived in the Underside for two years, yet in ch. 2 she answers Caelum's mention of Somnoria with "That's not a Remnian province… There's nothing outside of Remnia anymore." Both can be true only if she thinks of Shadowmere as something other than "outside": Remnian-adjacent, a lawless borderland, or not a nation at all. The page doesn't say which. OPEN · decide before any Melena POV scene names what the Underside is.

SPELLING FLAG

The Beta writes "Alleia" three times; TCD-027 writes "Alea." This is a fine-detail spelling choice the audit leaves open, not a geography question. Pick one before the Remnia map is reviewed.

Welcome to Remnia says seventeen provinces in its text, lists eighteen names in its table, and says the Elemental Court controls "8 of 17" while marking nine. The Beta's seventeen controls. Precise provincial positions wait for Danyelle's Remnia map (TCD-027); nothing here places a province on a compass.

SOURCETCD-025, TCD-027, TCD-041; FTE Beta chs. 2, 7, 12–13, 19, 22; Welcome to Remnia; Character Bible (Tiran); FTE 31–39 chs. 31, 38.

IV.3
POWER MAP · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Power and Jurisdiction

Replace "who's in charge" with a real power map.

Remnia doesn't have one ruler. It has several centers of authority that overlap, compete, and sometimes need each other. CANON TCD-041. The Crown is the highest public civil and dynastic authority and directly controls London Town and imperial affairs. Provincial Leaders administer their territories under titles that vary. The Provincial Leadership Board gives the provinces formal representation, and a Conqueror's one-year seat can change it. The Elemental Court is a separate political-magical bloc with influence over several provinces and over elemental policy, but not over London Town. The Sanctum is semi-autonomous in magical regulation, bloodlines, Tournament law, doctrine, security, and historical control. It isn't simply the Crown's agency, and it isn't legally omnipotent over the Crown either.

The practical consequence for drafting: when something goes wrong in FTE, the first question is never "who's in charge?" It's "whose jurisdiction is this, and who else thinks it's theirs?"

CONFLICT
Who leads Zenithia?

The Beta has Governor Anise. Welcome to Remnia has Countess Beatrice Montclair. The Character Bible makes Tiran heir to a Blackwood Dukedom of Zenithia. The Beta controls the on-page office; a duchy and a governorship can coexist under TCD-041's "titles vary", but no source says they do. Don't let Tiran exercise Zenithian authority until this is settled.

CONFLICT
London Town's royal house.

Welcome to Remnia: King William and Queen Dalia Augustine. Character Bible: Crown Princess Dalia and Prince Consort David Augustine. FTE 31–39: Dalia is "the Queen of London Town." All three are Supporting. TCD-041's direct Crown control of London Town is canon, so whatever the Augustines are, they rule under it.

IV.4
INSTITUTION DOSSIER · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

The Sanctum

Dossier for the institution behind most FTE secrets.

The Sanctum is the institution FTE is actually about. It regulates magic, bloodlines, Tournament law, doctrine, security, and historical control. CANON TCD-041. It runs the Tournament in public as a sacred tradition and a political reward, and in private uses it to classify, cultivate, control, exploit, monitor, suppress, or eliminate dangerous people. CANON TCD-034. It is the principal enforcer of Katherine's concealment and custodian of the records. CANON TCD-059.

It also doesn't get to be the villain of everything. The rulings are careful: the Sanctum accepts and may exploit the London Town forfeit but doesn't cause it (TCD-042, TCD-052), and it didn't originate Katherine's erasure (TCD-059). An institution that frightening is more frightening when it's also bounded.

What the Beta shows the Sanctum doing. (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT)

FunctionOn the page
MembershipMembers are "chosen from the highest ranks of each province, then stripped of their identities"; officials wear black and gold, and gray robes at the Maze

SurveillanceThe Houses' common areas are monitored around the clock, audio and visual; the Sanctum reviews all footage and broadcasts select clips

IV.4 · THE SANCTUMCONTINUED

Code Sixteen, as the Beta quotes it: "No Piece shall wield, develop, or demonstrate a second elemental affinity beyond the one registered at birth, whether innate, enhanced, or artificially induced. Penalty: immediate removal from the Tournament and Imperial discipline." "Imperial law didn't bother splitting hairs. If a second element answered when you called, you were in violation."

What the development chapters add. (SUPPORTED / PROVISIONAL · FTE 31–39) The Sanctum writes the Naming Ceremony scripts, which King Pieces "approve" and Queen Pieces read (ch. 32). Elite mages administer Heart's Mirror. Pieces are isolated before Challenge Six. Ceiling cameras show a tally light that never truly goes off. A no-kill order is issued for Challenge Six, then a non-verbal yield is ruled invalid and a surrendering Piece dies (ch. 39).

REVISION FLAG (DEVELOPMENT CHAPTERS)
The penalty for a second element.

FTE 31–39 ch. 38 says dualists have been illegal "since the Faction Wars" and that "the punishment for being a dualist was death." The Beta's Code Sixteen says removal from the Tournament and Imperial discipline. The Beta controls. "Imperial discipline" can be as dark as the book needs, but it has to be introduced as that, not as a death statute.

GUARDRAIL

"The Sanctum knows" is never a complete sentence. Which office, which official, since when, and on what evidence? An institution this compartmentalized can hold a secret in one room and be ignorant of it in the next.

IV.5
FACTION TABLE · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Elemental Magic

Teach the system before Katherine's exception.

Remnian magic is elemental, organized into factions, and registered at birth. It descends from the same primordial substrate as the other three lands' magic and is now its own system, with its own access, costs, and classifications. CANON TCD-035. Nothing in this section makes a Remnian element equivalent to a Somnorian or Underside practice (II.5).

The seven factions. The roster and descriptions come from FTE Elemental Factions (SUPPORTED). The right-hand column is what the Beta actually shows.

NOT THE SAME AS
Dawners and Flight.

In the Factions file, Dawners fly with their bodies and Flight flies with machines. Old Prosperity's Legion is "in the sky," but that doesn't make it a Dawner or Flight unit; Atlas is a Frost who commands it.

One element, registered at birth. The Beta treats an elemental affinity as something recorded when you're born and marked on the wrist. Code Sixteen forbids a second one "whether innate, enhanced, or artificially induced" (IV.4). An augmenter, "concentrated elemental compound, smuggled in from black-market labs," forces a second element into someone's system "for a few brilliant, illegal minutes"; Olivia Rembrandt uses one in ch. 12. Raven's suppressor rod and neutralizing cuffs shut elements down, and Tiran counters with an off-grid "patch" of old fire. "Dyads were illegal." (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT, ch. 12)

IV.6
SYSTEM EXPLAINER · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

The Tournament: Structure and Purpose

Explain both faces of the Tournament.

The Chess Tournament pits two provinces against each other with living Pieces on a chessboard's positions. This year it's Old Prosperity against London Town. The Empress's opening letter sets the public terms: thirty-two Pieces, "sixteen from each province, eight men and eight women"; the goal is to "capture the opposing King. Achieve Checkmate. Win."; and "to the victor, I offer a seat on the Provincial Leadership Board — a year of power, a year of favor, a year to reshape your province's future." (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT, front matter)

That's the public half. The private half is canon too: the Sanctum uses selection, assignments, challenges, stress, injuries, alliances, and survival to classify, cultivate, control, exploit, monitor, suppress, or eliminate dangerous people. CANON TCD-034. Neither half cancels the other. The Board seat is real, and so is the cull.

The Tournament is designed around seven Challenges, and reaching the Seventh isn't guaranteed. Attrition, Checkmate, disqualification, intervention, or another decisive outcome can end it early. In the current FTE, the Sanctum declares Challenge Six decisive and terminates the Tournament. CANON TCD-007

REVISION FLAG (FTE BETA)
"Six challenges."

The Empress's letter calls the Tournament "survival distilled into six challenges." TCD-007 makes the design seven, with the Sixth ending this one. The letter's wording needs revising to fit the ruling. It doesn't compete with it.

How it runs, as drafted.
StageOn the page
CandidacyTwenty is within the "Tournament candidate range"; High Command's order to Katherine arrives under General Gareth Thorne's signature
DelegationCandidates are assessed, then placed on the Board at a ceremony "by the power vested in the Sanctum"; Delegation members are exempt from the identity scan

SUPERSEDED

Welcome to Remnia's eight selections per province and its Dax-as-Rook are superseded by the Beta's sixteen and the Placements file's King's Knight. The Character Bible's "Elias was supposed to be the King" belongs to an older generation; Maxim is King.

NAMING FLAG

FTE 31–39's Naming Ceremony calls two London Town pawns "Sine Cauldiste" and "Jin Wang"; the Placements file has Sine Cauldren and Jin Voss. It also gives Ivy Lennox two ages (twenty and eighteen) and two younger siblings. Copyedit when the chapters are rewritten.

Why it matters. Every Tournament scene is performing for three audiences at once: the crowd, the provinces' politicians, and the Sanctum's evaluators. A Piece can win the broadcast and lose the evaluation. Katherine's exposure is exactly that kind of event.

Current challenge architecture.

Two challenges down, two wins: that's where Old Prosperity stands in ch. 21 as the Maze begins. (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT)

Challenge Two carries a cross-book event. One document is "The Breathbound Law, as recorded in the Imperial Archives, Year 847." The other is older, unmarked, with no seal and no cited year, and it carries lines from Somnoria's Dreamwalker's Writ: "Time is a spiral, not a line"; "The script was written, not the death"; "What sings in blood does not decide." Katherine notices it "felt related to the Breathbound Law," briefly suspects a draft from before "the Empire picked the lines it liked best," and dismisses the thought: "Conspiracy theories didn't win challenges." Old Prosperity wins by choosing the Breathbound Law. (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT, ch. 19)

That makes ch. 19 the first on-page moment where anyone holds two national descendants of the Shattered Veil side by side (II.13). The Companion reads what it shows and no more:

LaneWhat ch. 19 establishes
Character knowledgeKatherine registers resemblance and a possible editorial history, then rejects it. She doesn't know the second text is Somnorian.
Institutional / public versionThe Imperial record is the authentic one by every official test. The Sanctum's archive can produce the Writ-bearing text on demand.

Challenge Six as the development chapters draft it. The Sanctum announces mirrored single combat: "Victory is determined by yield, incapacitation, or death." Cordelia reports a Sanctum no-kill order and a crowd chanting for blood. In the first bout, Houston freezes Ellis's throat; Ellis signals surrender with an open palm; the Sanctum rules that "verbal confirmation is required"; the crowd chants FINISH; Houston kills him. (SUPPORTED · rewrite required) Before the bouts, Minho names Corporal Katherine his successor for the En Guard and Atlas names her for the Legion, and she refuses the premise. That scene sits directly on the command-authority rules in IV.20.

SUPERSEDED

The plan's Duel ending (Melena realizes the sisterhood mid-fight, burns away Katherine's emblems, Katherine performs the Prayer on Melena, Tiran finds Melena and the forfeit follows) conflicts with the staged reveal (TCD-050) and the command-authorized forfeit (TCD-042, TCD-052). Its Frost-severance beat is compatible with TCD-058 and is a live candidate for the climax POSSIBLE.

IV.8
RULES TABLE · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Conqueror Rules

Define the title correctly.

The Conqueror isn't the King, and isn't whoever lands the last hit. The Conqueror is the surviving Piece whose decisive sanctioned action earns victory, and that Piece holds the winning province's Provincial Leadership Board seat for the next year. CANON TCD-034. Under a forfeit, the Conqueror is the surviving Piece whose final sanctioned action or matchup directly produces the decisive state that causes the forfeit.

Katherine Nicholas is this Tournament's Conqueror. CANON TCD-034. Atlas and Tiran's confrontation is officially a draw by intervention; Katherine and Melena produce the decisive sequence.

SituationWho is ConquerorStatus
Ordinary CheckmateThe surviving Piece whose decisive sanctioned action earns it; not automatically the KingCANON TCD-034; the precise Checkmate definition OPEN
ForfeitThe surviving Piece whose final sanctioned action or matchup directly produces the decisive stateCANON TCD-034
This TournamentKatherine, because her sanctioned action against or for Melena prevents catastrophe, saves or stabilizes Melena, and creates the decisive stateCANON TCD-034, TCD-042
The last physical attack before an interventionDoesn't decide it; Atlas doesn't win by landing itCANON TCD-034
The Conqueror is dead or incapacitatedNot ruledOPEN

The Beta's own words fit the ruling: Old Prosperity's mess hall hangs banners "embroidered with the names of the Conquerors who had led Old Prosperity to glory," and Part Three of the Beta is titled THE CONQUEROR. Michelle Nicholas "secured one of Old Prosperity's greatest Tournament victories"; whether she was its Conqueror isn't stated.

GUARDRAIL

The Empress's letter says the goal is to capture the King. That's the public rule, and the Beta should keep saying it. It isn't how this Tournament ends, and the Conqueror rule already covers why.

The Factions file describes a 2092 ER Tournament whose last two survivors forfeited rather than accept victory, and an Empire that covered it up. If recovered, it would give the London Town forfeit an institutional memory. It isn't canon, and its survivor Zephyr Windrider has nothing to do with Zephyr Augustine.

IV.9
DOSSIER · PROTAGONIST · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Katherine Nicholas

Protagonist dossier.

Katherine is a soldier who has been told her whole life that she's exactly what she looks like: Michelle Nicholas's daughter, a Frost, a Corporal with a rank she has to keep earning. Every one of those things is true. None of them is complete. The book is the slow discovery that the fence around her life was built on purpose (ch. 13), and the Companion's job is to make sure she discovers it in the right order.

IV.10
COMPARISON SPREAD · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Katherine's Elemental Anatomy

Replace the Dyad label with the ruled anatomy.

This is the most carefully ruled magic in FTE, and the development chapters get it wrong in instructive ways, so read this before touching any scene where Katherine's element changes.

Katherine genuinely possesses Frost and Fire. CANON TCD-049. They aren't two ordinary channels. They're alternative expressions of one less-specialized ancestral Remnian elemental capacity, an older inheritance resurfacing in a modern body. From childhood that capacity organized itself around a dominant Frost expression, and Frost training reinforced it. CANON TCD-058. Ordinary stress and Fire exposure don't activate Fire. In FTE's climax, catastrophic severance or unavailability of her established Frost configuration lets the older capacity reroute through what modern Remnia calls Fire. No deliberate magical suppression is established.

Zephyr is the contrast case. He is a genuine modern Fire/Frost Dyad. CANON TCD-049. The Sanctum would likely call Katherine a Dyad too, because its taxonomy has no better word; that diagnosis is incomplete or wrong (II.15).

KatherineZephyr
Objective anatomyOne ancestral capacity; alternative expressionsTwo modern channels, Fire and Frost
Registered asFrostFlamer
Institutional label"Dyad" (likely; wrong or incomplete)"Dyad" (correct)
Before FTEFrost only, from childhoodTwo elements; 31–39 says since thirteen, after a training accident
On the pageExamination: Fire trauma, Frost response (ch. 7)Tundra: "orange fire edged with blue" in one hand, something else in the other; Melena sees both elements (ch. 18); Katherine sees them (ch. 17)
What opens the second expressionCatastrophic Frost failure, in the climaxAlready open

The Examination is the baseline. In ch. 7, Fire is on her body, the stress is extreme, her output spikes, and what comes out is Frost. The ruling was built on that scene. Any later scene in which ordinary fear, anger, heat, or grief pulls Fire out of her contradicts the drafted baseline, not just the ruling.

AUTHOR RULING, 2026-09-26
TCD-058 clarified (no new TCD number assigned).

CANON Heart's Mirror may foreshadow Katherine's hidden ancestral capacity through Fire imagery, fear, symbolic or ambiguous memory, lineage recognition, or other Mirror-generated material. Her Fire doesn't become objectively active during Challenge Five or its aftermath; she leaves the Mirror still operating through her Frost-dominant configuration. Material inside the Mirror isn't automatically an objective recovered memory. Her first objective Fire manifestation stays in the Challenge Six climax, after her established Frost pathway catastrophically fails or becomes unavailable and her broader capacity reroutes through the expression modern Remnia calls Fire.

What the ruling does to FTE 31–39. The rewrite rule is simple: whatever the Mirror shows her, Katherine walks out with Frost and only Frost.

CANON WHAT’S RULED

The Sanctum would call Katherine a Dyad. It’d be wrong. Zephyr is a modern Fire/Frost Dyad; Katherine is one older, less-specialized capacity that organized itself around Frost. Fire isn’t a second channel waiting behind a door. It’s where the capacity reroutes when the Frost configuration fails.

IV.11
LINEAGE · FIVE LAYERS · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Lineage: Biology, Law, Record, Succession

Keep four kinds of "family" in four columns.

One fact about Katherine's family is settled, and it doesn't settle anything else. Tobias Lockwood is the shared biological father of Katherine Nicholas and Melena Lockwood. Katherine's biological parents are Michelle Nicholas and Tobias; Melena's are Lucia Hastings-Lockwood and Tobias. They are paternal half-sisters. CANON TCD-040. That truth doesn't independently establish Katherine's legal status, her records, her legitimacy, her title, her succession rights, or what the public knows.

So the Companion keeps five layers apart, and every scene touching Katherine's parentage should know which layer it's operating in.

LayerQuestionCurrent answerStatus
BiologicalWho are her parents?Michelle and TobiasCANON TCD-040
LegalWhat is she under Remnian law: legitimate, illegitimate, royal, not royal?Not ruledOPEN

IV.11 · LINEAGE: BIOLOGY, LAW, RECORD, SUCCESSIONCONTINUED
CONFLICT
Melena's eyes, now with more evidence.

The Beta gives her "Lockwood amber eyes" and says Cordelia shares them. 3SL gives her green. FTE 31–39 has Tobias's eyes "the same shade as hers" and green. The Character Bible says emerald. The Remnian Timeline Before Story Start says green "associated with the Hastings lineage" and gives Elias golden Lockwood eyes; the unaudited Imperial Lineage repeats it. The Companion preserves the conflict (III.20). The Beta controls FTE's drafted page; nobody has ruled which book changes.

REVISION FLAG (DEVELOPMENT CHAPTERS)
The parents' elements.

One Mirror line in 31–39 makes Tobias a Frost and Lucia a Flamer. The Beta has it the other way round: Lucia was "one of the most powerful" Frosts in the empire, Cordelia inherited her Frost ("Second generation"), and Melena "took after their father — fire running through her veins instead of ice" (ch. 8). The Beta controls; the Mirror lines need revising. Neither version says which side Katherine's ancestral capacity comes from (TCD-049). A parent's element isn't evidence of an inherited anatomy.

Succession as the sources frame it. Welcome to Remnia describes a strictly matrilineal Hastings succession and a Crown that named Elias heir after Lucia's death to preserve its image. 31–39 has a Privy Council push Elias into the role. The Remnian Timeline Before Story Start (repeated in the unaudited Imperial Lineage) has Lucia decree at Melena's birth that daughters keep their place ahead of younger brothers. None of this is ruled. The one drafted fact is that Melena is second in line.

GUARDRAIL

Proving Tobias's paternity (IV.13) doesn't make Katherine a princess, a Lockwood, or an heir. Each of those is a separate decision, and some of them are the Crown's to make in-story.

SOURCETCD-032, TCD-040, TCD-049, TCD-059; FTE Beta front matter, chs. 2, 8, 12; Welcome to Remnia; Character Bible; FTE 31–39 chs. 31, 33, 35; FTE Remnian Timeline Before Story Start; FTE Remnian Timeline Early; FTE 2025 FINAL and Full 4 Series Flesh Out (legacy, corroboration only); The Imperial Lineage (unaudited, comparison only). Record: Remnian Royal Lineage (the working record follows this section) + Katherine Secrecy/Concealment Architecture.

IV.11
WORKING RECORD · 1 OF 48 · REMNIAN IMPERIAL + ROYAL LINEAGE

Remnian Lineage: The Working Record

Rebuilt from the evidence, 2026-10-09. A derived record, not a source.

This record rebuilds the Remnian imperial and royal lineage from the sources, claim by claim. Each line names the source it comes from and carries the status that source earns under the authority order: rulings, then the Beta, then current author decisions, development, reference, shared lore, supporting artifacts, and legacy. It isn’t a source itself, and it settles nothing a ruling leaves open.

Five layers stay apart, as IV.11 requires: biological, legal family status, public and recorded, title, and succession. A parent never fills a succession slot, and a title never proves a parent. Where two sources disagree, both stay on the page with their sources, and the disagreement gets a number (LC-nn) and a working block in lane D.

LOCKED ANCHOR · CANON TCD-040

Tobias Lockwood is the biological father of Katherine (with Michelle Nicholas) and of Melena (with Lucia Hastings-Lockwood). Katherine and Melena are paternal half-sisters.

That doesn’t make Katherine legally acknowledged, a Lockwood by law, a princess, publicly known, or an heir. TCD-059 governs the concealment only.

GUARDRAIL
Agreement between two weak sources isn’t canon. Two timelines that agree are still two timelines.

Conflict Ledger

Where the sources disagree, and what would settle it.
CLAIMSOURCE ASOURCE BHIGHER-AUTHORITY EVIDENCECURRENT STATUSWHAT WOULD NEED TO CHANGE
LC-01
Melena’s eye colour		Beta: “Lockwood amber eyes,” shared by Cordelia. F2025 also has amber.BSS: “eyes as green as emeralds,” a Hastings trait. CB: emerald. 3SL: green. 31–39: green, Tobias’s eyes “the same shade.” IL repeats BSS.Beta controls FTE prose (TCD-001). No ruling on which book changes.CONFLICT
CONFLICT		An author ruling on the colour, naming which manuscript revises (Beta or 3SL).
LC-02
Aracelia’s surname and house		Beta letter: “Aracelia Meson Mignonette.” BSS: “Aracelia Meson Mignonette Hastings.” TLE: “Aracelia Mignonette Hastings.”31–39: “Empress Aracelia Lockwood.”The Beta’s styling controls the drafted page. No ruling on her house; WTR, BSS and TLE all put her in the Hastings line.CONFLICT
CONFLICT (name) · house Hastings SUPPORTED		A ruling on her full styled name and house; then the 31–39 line is revised.
LC-03
Lucia’s birth year		BSS: born 2047 (47 at her death on 7 Sept 2094).TLE: born 26 Oct 2055, elder twin of Charlotte, to Aracelia (17) and Jackson Harper.None. TCD-010: no existing timeline controls chronology. Both files give Aracelia’s birth as 2038, so BSS’s 2047 would make Aracelia nine at Lucia’s birth.CONFLICT
CONFLICT		The new canonical Remnian timeline (TCD-010) fixes Aracelia’s and Lucia’s births together.
