IV.12
CONCEALMENT MATRIX · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Katherine's Concealment

Assign each layer of the secret to its owner.

Katherine wasn't hidden by one person. Each layer of the concealment has its own owner. CANON TCD-059

LayerOwnerWhat they controlEvidence on the pageOpen
Public and legal erasure; dynastic exclusionLucia / the Lockwood CrownThe decision that Katherine is not acknowledgedNone draftedMotive; Aracelia's role
Enforcement; records; surveillanceThe SanctumWhat the files say and who can see themNone specific to Katherine yetExact record alterations; the file's current contents
Non-acknowledgmentTobiasHis silence; he's not established as the architectNone draftedConsent or coercion
Raising and protection inside the constraintsMichelleKatherine's daily life; the fenceThe travel ban and its ready excuses (ch. 13)What she was told she could and couldn't do; why

Michelle is the only owner with a drafted footprint, and it's the right one. She didn't design the erasure; she managed a daughter inside it. The fence in ch. 13 is protection that looks like caution. The reader should be able to reread every Michelle excuse later and see a woman keeping her child out of rooms where someone might look at her too closely.

What the development chapters add about Michelle. (SUPPORTED · 31–39 ch. 31) Michelle and Tobias "tried to make it work for years before it all fell apart." Queen Dalia "was one of three people who knew about Michelle and Tobias," and after Lucia and Tobias died she sat with Michelle night after night, then sent her sons Maxim and Minho when she couldn't come herself. Michelle can't tell the room "that she wasn't just some bastard captain's daughter." The chapter doesn't say that Dalia knows Katherine is Tobias's child; it says she knew about the relationship.

IV.12 · KATHERINE'S CONCEALMENTCONTINUED
OPEN, WITH A SOURCE SPREAD
Was Katherine's birth ever public?

TCD-059 leaves open whether it was publicly known before suppression or never meaningfully acknowledged. The sources pull in different directions:

The Remnian Timeline Before Story Start describes a "highly publicized and controversial birth" followed by a royal decree making any discussion of it treason, and, in 2091, a Sanctum suppression order; the unaudited Imperial Lineage repeats the birth and the decree. The Character Bible speaks of royal decrees and "whispered scandals." Welcome to Remnia says she was raised with "a name that wasn't hers and a lineage no one dared to say out loud," and that acknowledging her would mean "admitting Tobias violated Imperial marriage law." The audit flags that file's public-scandal framing as unresolved.

Nothing in the Beta shows any character treating Katherine as a known scandal. Do not smooth this into "everyone half-knew." It's a real choice with real consequences for how people have looked at Katherine for twenty years.

Who knows what, at FTE's opening.

IV.13
EVIDENCE CHAIN · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

The Sister Reveal

Stage the reveal across evidence, not exposition.

No single moment tells the sisters the truth. The reveal is built in four stages, and each stage has a different kind of evidence. CANON TCD-050

StageWhat happensWho drives itKind of evidenceCan it prove paternity?
1. InvestigationMelena finds inconsistencies before Challenge Six, because 3SL destroyed her trust in official history. By the Duel she suspects Katherine is biologically connected to the Lockwoods or Tobias.MelenaDocumentary and circumstantialNo
2. ConfrontationIn the climax, magical, behavioral, and inherited recognition reinforces her suspicion. Melena tells or confronts Katherine directly.MelenaMagical and behavioralNo
3. VerificationAuthenticated lineage, birth, or Sanctum evidence proves Tobias's paternity.OPENAuthenticated recordYes
4. Personal confirmationMichelle supplies the human history and the concealment.MichelleTestimonyConfirms; explains

Katherine's reason for saving Melena comes from her own moral arc, not only from sisterhood. CANON TCD-050. The Beta has already built that arc (IV.9).

IV.13 · THE SISTER REVEALCONTINUED
DRAFTING GAP

The ruling needs Melena investigating before Challenge Six. This read of chs. 1–29 found no suspicion drafted yet, and 31–39 doesn't draft it either. Stage 1 has to be planted in the unwritten half, early enough that her suspicion at the Duel is earned rather than announced.

SUPERSEDED

The Challenges FTE Year has Melena realize the sisterhood mid-fight from a magical reaction. One reaction can't carry the reveal (TCD-050). Recognition in the Duel reinforces what she already suspects.

GUARDRAIL

Resemblance is a clue, not a finding. When Michelle or Dalia sees Tobias in Melena's face (31–39), that's their knowledge and their grief. It doesn't move either sister forward a single stage.

OPEN DEVELOPMENT · THE EVIDENCE CHAIN · OPEN
FIXEDFour stages in order; Melena's suspicion before the Duel; confrontation in the climax; authenticated verification; Michelle's testimony (TCD-050); concealment owners (TCD-059).
UNDECIDEDMelena's first clue and where she finds it; what she already knows from Shadowmere; which record verifies; who authenticates it; when Michelle testifies; whether the reveal goes public, and when.
WHY IT MATTERSMelena is the investigator. If her clues aren't on the page, the reveal happens to her instead of because of her.
DECIDE BYThe first clue before the next Melena chapter is drafted.

IV.14
DOSSIER · PROTAGONIST · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Melena Lockwood

Dossier that protects her from villain framing.

Melena comes into FTE two years after Remy died in front of her, carrying guilt for a death a gang leader caused (TCD-063) and a Fire she still can't hold. She isn't the villain of this book, and nothing in FTE may make her one. CANON TCD-009. She can make destructive, dangerous, even catastrophic choices, but they have to grow from the woman 3SL built.

Every one of those beats sits well inside TCD-009: dangerous ambition, never villainy.

IV.15
LEVERAGE LEDGER · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Caelum and Melena: The Leverage Ledger

Track a two-sided transactional alliance.

Melena and Caelum are in a transactional alliance with coercive leverage on both sides. CANON TCD-043. He isn't her handler and she isn't his subordinate. "Asset" is his operational language, and the Companion's narration never adopts it (II.15). He is an independent cross-land actor with deep Nightmare-adjacent access and nightmare magic, and he is not a member of the Nightmare organization. CANON TCD-031

How it starts (ch. 2). A hooded man in Shadowmere knows a name Melena has told no one. He has silver hair "not dyed," skin like wheat in a Rovencian field, three tiny studs in his ears, each a different metal, and violet eyes. He tells her Elias is in Somnoria as Professor Alexander Thorne, that his cousin Cassius is investigating him, and that the borders are closed "but there are still ways through." He wants her in the Tournament, win or lose, "as long as you keep their eyes on you." The price she gets is direct access to Elias's messages. (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT)

IV.16
MECHANICS EXPLAINER · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Atlas: The Synchronization Injury

Fix the origin; keep the rest open.

Atlas's black-vein deterioration begins in the Conduit Trial. CANON TCD-051. He force-synchronizes his dense, controlled Frost with Katherine's reactive Frost in the same circuit. He tries to impose structure; the Frost overcorrects, turns inward, and creates a progressive injury visible as black veins. It isn't an accumulation of ordinary Element Rot, and no earlier Sanctum corruption, artifact, or experiment caused it.

What's on the page.
SourceStatusWhat it shows
Beta ch. 28CURRENT DRAFTED TEXTNico briefs the Trial: four pillars, bonded-pair synchronization, runes in white and black. By the chapter's end the black veins reach Atlas's cheekbones.
Chapter 29.docxSUPPORTED · rewrite requiredAtlas collapses; his skin is "cold in a way frost shouldn't be — wrong temperature, wrong texture"; black veining creeps toward his eyes and throat. Katherine performs the Elders' Prayer; the veining freezes "mid-crawl," then retreats down his forearms "until it thinned into nothing."
FTE 31–39SUPPORTED · rewrite requiredA fading "tether" between Katherine and Atlas, carrying his alarm and thoughts, and Dax's heat; Atlas pale, hollow-eyed, hands bandaged
Character BibleSUPPORTED"The most powerful Frost in Old Prosperity"; control "near-absolute"; the room froze at his birth

The Character Bible's Atlas is the reason the ruling works. A Frost defined by control is exactly the Frost who would try to impose structure on someone else's reactive power and get hurt doing it.

FTE › IV.16 ATLAS: THE SYNCHRONIZATION INJURY · CONTINUES →IV.16 / 1 · 163
SPINE
3SL
FTE
ADST
TSWK
CONTINUITY
IV.16 · ATLAS: THE SYNCHRONIZATION INJURYCONTINUED
GUARDRAIL
No retrofitted history.

Atlas's past violence (in ch. 7 Katherine recalls that he nearly killed Minho three years earlier; the Character Bible has a battle that nearly destroyed a mountainside) is not an earlier corruption, and an early description of his veins standing out against his skin is not foreshadowing of a pre-existing condition. The injury starts in the Trial.

OPEN
Does the injury come back?

TCD-051 calls it progressive, and the audit asks why it progresses after synchronization ends. Chapter 29.docx has the Prayer drive it out entirely. Both can't be the whole story: either the Prayer stabilizes it without curing it, or it returns, or the progression happens before the Prayer. The Prayer restores a viable magical pattern and can't regenerate destroyed tissue (TCD-057), which leaves room for lasting damage.

IV.17
PERFORMANCE RECORD · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

The Elders' Prayer in FTE

Record Katherine's performance.

Katherine performs the Elders' Prayer for Atlas. CANON TCD-009. It's the same inherited working Selah performs in 3SL, in a Remnian form, about two years later and with no causal link between the two performances. CANON TCD-023, TCD-048. Its function is fixed: it restores a living person's viable magical pattern when that pattern is severed, corrupted, or catastrophically dysregulated, and it can call someone back from active dying. It can't resurrect completed death or regenerate destroyed anatomy. CANON TCD-057 (II.14)

The controlling generation hasn't drafted it. Chapter 29.docx has, as a candidate (SUPPORTED · rewrite required), and it gets a great deal right.

The candidate scene. Atlas collapses in the Conduit Trial. Katherine laces her fingers through his and presses their palms flat, "forcing contact the way her mother had taught her. Press here. Hold here. Eyes closed. Don't let go." Her Frost pours into him and doesn't behave like her power: "It didn't bite outward. It went into him." His Frost "reached back. Barely." Lines write themselves up both their forearms, matching, permanent. The black veining stops, then retreats. The Prayer takes long enough that London Town claims its second conduit, and Old Prosperity loses the Challenge.

The words, as the candidate draft has them. This is manuscript text in a rewrite-required file, not canon wording:

By Elder's right. By covenant sworn— / I stake my claim. / Frost to frost, I bind your name— / For life we'll share. Through better or worse— / Let death know my name first.

Checked against the rulings.
Element of the candidateRulingVerdict
Atlas is actively dying of magical dysregulation; the Prayer stops the veiningTCD-051, TCD-057Compatible: a viable pattern restored at the dying threshold
Michelle taught Katherine the contactTCD-023 (transmission open)Compatible; it would make Michelle a link in the Remnian transmission chain POSSIBLE

The public version, as the candidate draft stages it. The Prayer happens on live broadcast. The announcers call it "an ancient Frost binding ritual. Once begun, it cannot be stopped," and "something we have never seen before in a Tournament"; the crowd chants KATHERINE! ATLAS! That's exactly the kind of institutional misreading II.15 tracks: Remnia's public knows it as a Frost bond, and its objective function is broader. If this staging survives, the Prayer stops being a family secret the moment Katherine performs it, and every viewer who knows the old rite now knows she knows it.

COPYEDIT FLAG (FTE DEVELOPMENT FILE)

Chapter 29.docx writes "Elder's Prayer" and spells her name "Nicolas" in the announcers' lines. The Companion spelling is Elders' Prayer; her name is Nicholas.

OPEN
Is there a second performance?

TCD-057 leaves the Prayer's effects "on Atlas/Melena" open, and the audit's open issues include "Katherine–Melena sanctioned action/prayer mechanics." The Challenges plan has Katherine perform it on Melena in the Duel. Whether Katherine's decisive act for Melena is a Prayer, and what a second performance would cost her, isn't ruled.

IV.19
DOSSIER · ARC WORKSHEET · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Minho Augustine

Hold space for an unfinished arc.

Minho's death is not assumed. CANON TCD-009. The legacy draft killed him, and the ruling retired that: his current arc has to be found first, and death may only return if it becomes earned and indispensable to his own arc or to Tandem's consequences. That makes Minho the least-settled major character in FTE and the one most at risk of being written by momentum.

What the sources establish.

Minho under pressure, as drafted. In the tundra he refuses Katherine's search, then relents: "Bring him back." She reads it as fear for Zephyr he can't say out loud. At the Maze, he goes rigid when he realizes Maxim, a Terrain, is walking into a maze built to suppress Terrain. (CURRENT DRAFTED TEXT, chs. 17, 21) In 31–39 (SUPPORTED) he takes command the moment he sees Katherine's wrists, orders the lens blinded, calls her a Dyad, turns on Sebastian for hiding Zephyr, and names her his successor to the En Guard.

Minho arc worksheet. Pre-filled where the sources speak.

IV.20
CAUSAL CHAIN · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

The Endgame: Forfeit and Command Authority

Lock the causal chain.

Old Prosperity wins because London Town forfeits after Challenge Six. CANON TCD-009. The forfeit is an act of agency on three levels at once, and the rulings keep the levels apart. CANON TCD-042, TCD-052

The causal chain. Every link is ruled; none is drafted.

#LinkWhoRuling
1Katherine's Frost configuration catastrophically fails; the older capacity reroutes as FireKatherineTCD-058
2Katherine's final sanctioned action against or for Melena prevents catastrophe and saves or stabilizes MelenaKatherineTCD-042
3That action creates the decisive stateKatherineTCD-034, TCD-042
4Melena consciously chooses survival over victory, rather than sacrificing more people to reach Challenge SevenMelenaTCD-042, TCD-052
5Tiran advises, supports, and operationalizes her choice; he can't surrender the province on his own authorityTiranTCD-042, TCD-052
6Melena brings the decision to Maxim, or endorses it to himMelena → MaximTCD-052
7Maxim, alive, conscious, and acting as King, formally enters or authorizes the forfeitMaximTCD-052
8The Sanctum accepts and formalizes it; it may exploit the outcome but doesn't compel itThe SanctumTCD-042, TCD-052
9Katherine is Conqueror—TCD-034

Atlas and Tiran's confrontation happens in the same climax and is officially a draw by intervention. CANON TCD-034. The sister reveal's confrontation stage lands here too (IV.13).

Tournament Command Authority. What each rank can do, as far as the rulings go.

RankCanCan'tOpen
KingFormally enter or authorize a provincial forfeit (Maxim)—Whether the King alone holds that power in every case

The SanctumAccept, verify, formalizeBe the original cause of this forfeitIts veto
NOT THE SAME AS
Tournament command and military command.

In 31–39 ch. 39, Minho names Corporal Katherine his successor for the En Guard and Atlas names her for the Legion, citing a protocol that lets a commanding officer name a successor regardless of rank. That's Old Prosperity's military chain, not Tournament authority. It doesn't give Katherine the power to forfeit or accept a forfeit, and it doesn't change who formally acts for London Town.

SUPERSEDED

The legacy ending centered Tiran's announcement and incapacitated Maxim to make room for it. Retired by TCD-042 and TCD-052. Melena as the breaking villain who hands Katherine the win is retired by TCD-009.

Why Challenge Seven matters here. Melena's choice is a choice against something. The reader has to know what continuing costs: which Pieces would have to die, what the Sanctum has promised or threatened, what the Seventh would have been. Without that, the forfeit reads as collapse instead of agency (IV.7).

IV.22
KNOWLEDGE LEDGER · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

Knowledge Progression

Who knows what, when, across the book.

FTE is two women learning, from opposite directions, that the official story about their family is wrong. It's also an Empire learning, in public, that one of its Queens has an element she shouldn't. Those are different knowledge problems, and the ledger keeps them separate.

Seed ledger. Dated to where it's shown; nothing is promoted to a whole society.

KNOWLEDGE FLAG
Who in Remnia knows other lands exist?

Melena's ch. 2 belief says a princess who has toured the provinces didn't. Cordelia in 31–39 speaks of Elias vanishing "into a foreign country." The Remnian Timeline Before Story Start (repeated in the unaudited Imperial Lineage) gives the heir apparent the traditional title "Duke of Somnoria" "as a means of fostering relations with the neighboring empire." Those three can't all describe the same public. TCD-016 says awareness varies by class, institution, and time; it doesn't say how it varies in Remnia. OPEN · decide before any Remnian character names Somnoria on-page again.

GUARDRAIL

Author knowledge doesn't travel into FTE heads. Katherine doesn't know she's Tobias's daughter, doesn't know what her magic objectively is, and doesn't know Selah exists. Melena doesn't know Katherine is her sister until her own investigation gives her reason to suspect it.

IV.24
ENDGAME PACKET · PART IV · FOR THEIR ENTERTAINMENT / REMNIA

FTE Endgame Packet

Pages beside the manuscript while chs. 30–39 are rewritten.

These are the pages that sit open beside the Duel. The rulings have fixed more of FTE's ending than of any other book's, so this packet is mostly non-negotiables and a short list of blanks.

Stop points. The Beta stops in ch. 29 with Melena in the med bay after Challenge Four, told she's "the third most powerful person in Remnia." The development chapters stop after the Duel's first bout, with Katherine in the viewing box watching Houston kill Ellis. Everything between the two is provisional.

Conditions at the Beta's stop point.
CharacterCondition
AtlasBlack veins to his cheekbones from the Conduit Trial (ch. 28)
MelenaIn the med bay (ch. 29)
KatherineFrost only; lover of Atlas since ch. 26; has seen Zephyr's two elements
Old ProsperityWins in Challenges One and Two; Beckett dead
London TownIvy dead; penalized in Challenge Two

CONTINUITY
IV.24 · FTE ENDGAME PACKETCONTINUED
Non-negotiables
  • Katherine's magic. Frost-dominant until the climax. Fire comes only when her Frost configuration catastrophically fails, and never from stress, heat, grief, or Melena. She's not Zephyr's kind of Dyad, whatever the Sanctum calls her. Heart's Mirror can foreshadow; she walks out of it Frost-dominant, and nothing inside it is proof of her past. (TCD-049, TCD-058; author ruling, 2026-09-26)
  • The reveal. Melena suspects before the Duel, from her own investigation. The Duel confirms nothing. Records prove it; Michelle explains it. (TCD-050)
  • Katherine's reason. She saves or stabilizes Melena from her own moral arc, not only because they're sisters. (TCD-050)
  • The forfeit. Melena chooses; Tiran supports; Maxim, alive and conscious, authorizes; the Sanctum accepts. (TCD-042, TCD-052)
  • The title. Katherine is Conqueror. Atlas and Tiran draw by intervention. (TCD-034)
  • Atlas. The injury started in the Trial. No earlier cause. (TCD-051)
  • The Prayer. Restores a viable pattern; no resurrection, no regeneration, no echo of Selah. (TCD-048, TCD-057)
  • Melena. Not the villain. (TCD-009)
  • Minho. Not assumed dead. (TCD-009)
  • Remy. The gang leader killed him. Melena's guilt is real and forensically wrong. (TCD-063)
  • The count. The Tournament was built for seven; the Sanctum ends it at Six. (TCD-007)

APPENDIX · STATUS & PROVENANCE SHEET

Canon Decision Log: FTE Slice

Every author ruling this volume cites, reproduced from the audit’s Canon Decision Log.

60 rulings are cited in this volume. Each row is reproduced from the Tandem Source Authority Audit — Working Record (Sep 26, 2026). Rulings this volume doesn’t cite appear in the full Companion’s Appendix A.

ID · DATETOPIC · BOOKSAUTHOR RULINGSUPERSEDES · DOWNSTREAM
TCD-001
2026-09-04		Controlling FTE manuscript generation
FTE/Shared		Beta is the controlling prose generation. Chapters 31–39 are usable new-development material, but all content outside the Beta must be rewritten/integrated before it becomes canon.SUPERSEDES FTE 2025 FINAL as prose authority; all older manuscript generations
DOWNSTREAM Future FTE audits distinguish usable planning/development from canonized prose. No unwritten Beta-era ending is assumed canon.
TCD-002
2026-09-04		3SL authority beyond drafted ch. 22

TCD-045
2026-09-24		Historical nature of the Divide
All four		The Divide is a two-stage historical crisis. First, a non-deliberate natural, celestial, cosmic, or magical rupture destabilized the primordial system and relations among the lands. Ancient peoples then created, adapted, bound, activated, or repurposed seals, artifacts, routes, living systems, and boundary structures in response. Their deliberate response did not cause the rupture, but it hardened its consequences into long-term separation. “Divide” may name the initiating catastrophe or the broader rupture–containment–hardening era. Long isolation accelerated specialization of one shared magical ancestry; migration may be a consequence, not the cause.SUPERSEDES Divide as solely migration; solely deliberate partition; solely natural catastrophe with no containment response; origin of four unrelated magics
DOWNSTREAM Build the Divide Reconstruction. TCD-055 now establishes the Drowning as later and distinct, and First Seal as later local containment. Rupture cause, continent/Prism chronology, Leviathan’s pre-First-Seal role, boundary intent, and duration remain open.
TCD-046
2026-09-24		Objective definition of Convergence
All four		Convergence is the progressive destabilization and re-entanglement of the four lands, magical branches, boundary systems, buried shared history, routes, artifacts, and entities after enforced separation. It can manifest through permeability, resonance, cross-land responses, restored contact, knowledge leakage, and failing isolation structures. It is a transition state, neither inherently repair nor destruction; present choices determine whether the result is controlled reconnection, transformed partial separation, restabilization, catastrophic collapse, or another state. It does not physically merge continents or flatten distinct magics. The Shattered Veil predicts compatible conditions, but its reliability is not yet fixed.SUPERSEDES Convergence as automatic salvation, apocalypse, continental merger, uniform magic, Echo Gate event, or any inherited legacy trigger/ending
DOWNSTREAM TCD-056 fixes long degradation/latent symptoms and late-ADST cross-system threshold, not a one-event origin. Build the causal timeline, symptom ledger, response clock, outcome tree, and knowledge states; precise onset and end state remain open.
